      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Glassworking is the process of turning sand into glass. To start, you must create a Glass Batch, of which there are four types: 1. Silica, from white sand. 2. Hematitic, from yellow, red, or pink sand. 3. Olivine, from green or brown sand. 4. Volcanic, from black sand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Silica Glass Batch",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Glass batches can then be crafted using one of the aforementioned colors of sand, plus Lime and a type of Potash.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Lime is one of the ingredients required to make glass batches. It is a powder obtained by heating Flux.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "A type of Potash or equivalent is also required for glass batches. Soda Ash can be used, which is a powder made from heating Dried Seaweed or Kelp. Saltpeter can be used as well.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Glassworking is done by starting with a glass batch, and then completing a series of steps. These steps may require specific tools: - A Blowpipe, to Blow and Stretch - A Paddle, to Flatten - Jacks, to Pinch - A Gem Saw, to Saw",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Tools of the Trade",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "The most important tool is the Blowpipe. It can be knapped from clay, and then fired into a Ceramic Blowpipe.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Ceramic blowpipes are brittle, and have a chance to to break when used. A more sturdy blowpipe can be worked from a Brass Rod on an anvil.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "The Flatten operation can be done with a Paddle, which is crafted from wood.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "The Pinch operation can be done with Jacks, made from welding two brass rods together.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "The Saw operation can be done with a Gem Saw. The gem saw is also used to break both Glass Blocks and Glass Panes and obtain them.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "First, glass on the blowpipe must be heated to Faint Red. Then, hold the blowpipe in your offhand and hold to perform each step. Use to pick up a hot blowpipe into your offhand. Blow Use the Blowpipe while facing straight ahead. Stretch Use the Blowpipe while facing straight down.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "How to Glass",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Flatten Use the Blowpipe while holding a Paddle in your main hand. Pinch Use the Blowpipe while holding Jacks in your main hand. Saw Use the Blowpipe while holding a Gem Saw in your main hand. Roll Use the Blowpipe with a Wool Cloth in your main hand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {

          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "The Pinch operation can be done with Jacks, made from welding two brass rods together.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "The Saw operation can be done with a Gem Saw. The gem saw is also used to break both Glass Blocks and Glass Panes and obtain them.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "First, glass on the blowpipe must be heated to Faint Red.Then, hold the blowpipe and hold to perform each step. Blow Use the Blowpipe while facing straight ahead. Stretch Use the Blowpipe while facing straight down.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "How to Glass",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Flatten Use the Blowpipe while holding a Paddle in your offhand. Pinch Use the Blowpipe while holding Jacks in your offhand. Saw Use the Blowpipe while holding a Gem Saw in your offhand. Roll Use the Blowpipe with a Wool Cloth in your offhand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        }

    {
      "id": "tfc:crops",
      "label": "Crops",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",
      "confidence": 0.7,
      "support": 125,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Soul soil may be tilled to form Soul Farmland. Crops work a little differently when you are Beneath. There are five nutrients: Death, Destruction, Decay, Sorrow, and Flame. Nutrients are viewed by holding a hoe, but will not appear unless they have a value greater than zero.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crops have a preferred nutrient: Instead of consuming it, this is the nutrient they will add to over time. The crop will happily consume the other four nutrients in order to grow. Nether crops cannot die, but still need block light to grow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are as follows: Sulfur: 20% decay, 10% flame Gunpowder: 60% Destruction, 10% Flame, 40% Death Ghast Tear: 30% Sorrow Blaze Powder: 20% Flame",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are also available in obtainable 'pure' forms from chest loot. Crafting these together yields Agonizing Fertilizer, which has them all in a neat package.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crimson Roots (Decay) and Warped Roots (Destruction) are growable as crops. These are most useful as means of improving the nutrient quality of your soul farmland. Nether Wart (Death), found in fortresses, can also be grown as a crop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The Gleamflower (Flame) can be grown as a crop, and also found in the world as a plant. It is useful as an infinite light source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Ghost Peppers (Sorrow) are an edible vegetable that can be grown in the Beneath!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "beneath:ghost_pepper",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "0.5 / 16.0g.",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Ghost Pepper",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "beneath:ghost_pepper",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Expires on: 7:11 July 2, 1000 (in 1 month(s) and 0 day(s))",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Ghost Pepper",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Curious Blocks",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Soul Clay",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.name",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Soul Clay may be discovered in soul sand valleys. It is a source of regular clay.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Sulfur can be broken with a shovel. Be careful, though: iron-containing items (including steel) explode in contact with sulfur!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Cutting grass will yield you warped or crimson straw. This can be made into thatch, the normal way.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "The blackstone aqueduct allows lava to be conducted at any distance.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Hellbricks are a building block that is also used for the Hellforge.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Blackstone bricks are made like regular bricks, and that includes needing to create the brick item.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge is a gratuitously large forge, with high capacity for fuel, products, and heating. It is constructed from Hellbricks, Anthracite, and Pyroxenite Aqueducts.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge requires lava to flow in aqueducts in its four corners, as well as 21 hellbricks. The 3x3 center must be filled with Anthracite piles, which then may be lit to start the forge.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The item slots of the Hellforge may contain fuel, or things to be smelted; there is no distinction. Be careful what you put in! There are also slots for melting things, as with the charcoal forge. The lava meter on the screen indicates temperature (as well as the visual movement of its items in world).",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge is a gratuitously large forge, with high capacity for fuel, products, and heating. It is constructed from Hellbricks, Cursecoal, and Blackstone Aqueducts.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge requires lava to flow in aqueducts in its four corners, as well as 21 hellbricks. The 3x3 center must be filled with Cursecoal piles, which then may be lit to start the forge.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The item slots of the Hellforge may contain fuel, or things to be smelted; there is no distinction. Be careful what you put in! There are also slots for melting things, as with the charcoal forge. The lava meter on the screen indicates temperature (as well as the visual movement of its items in world).",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Ores and Minerals",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "The ores you can find Beneath are mostly similar to what you can find on the surface, but with a few changes. Consult the Ores in TFG category for exact details.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "The most common rock type found is Migmatite. This deep metamorphic rock contains a wide variety of ores you'd normally find in other rock types on the surface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "In wetter areas, you may find Travertine. It is a sedimentary rock that can contain the same kind of ores as Migmatite.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Around the deepest level of the Beneath, the different rock types gradually all turn into Pyroxenite. This ultramafic igneous rock is extremely rich in ores, but this depth also comes with extreme heat, so be prepared!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Anthracite is a powerful fuel. It can be found in Migmatite in the upper half of the Beneath.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Ores and Minerals",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Beneath the overworld, you will still find ore veins to exploit. It is important to note that Netherrack does not collapse, like raw stone would. However, Blackstone, Nether Bricks, Cobblerack and miscellaneous other blocks do. Prospecting will still work Beneath the overworld.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Gold",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Gold replaces netherrack, and is common. Beneath lava, there is more gold, and richer.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Cursecoal is a powerful fuel. It replaces netherrack above level 80.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",

          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",

          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Quartz",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Quartz replaces netherrack at all levels, and is common.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Slimed Netherrack",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Slimed Netherrack occasionally replaces Netherrack above lava level.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "Dugout Canoes",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "The Dugout Canoe will likely be the first step on your aquatic journey! If you're in the copper age, you can probably build a canoe.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "You can put blocks or animals in the pilot seat, but be aware that there's no way to paddle the boat if you do this! It's most useful if you just want to send materials down a river on their own. A canoe has two seats, and is piloted from the rear. You need to hold a canoe paddle in order to move at full speed, and if two players are holding paddles, you can go even faster.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "To build a Dugout Canoe, place 3 stripped logs in a row, horizontally, and cut off the tops with a saw. Then, carve out the canoe shape with an axe. Finally, light the inside of the canoe on fire to hollow out the hull. It will take the same amount of time as a pit kiln to complete the hollowing process.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "Carving out a Dugout Canoe requires a softer wood, and as a result, only some logs are workable: Douglas Fir Pine Palm Spruce Sequoia Kapok Mangrove White Cedar Willow",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "Just a Log",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.name",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "The first step in making a Dugout Canoe.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "Weird Slabs",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.name",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "The top of the log has been removed using a saw.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "A Strange Sculpture",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.name",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "A fully carved Dugout Canoe, ready for hollowing out the hull.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "Don't forget to craft a paddle or two!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat.json",
          "text": "So, you've reached the Bronze Age and you want an upgrade? Well, the rowboat is for you. It has three seats, so it can hold 3 players, or up to 2 animals, including horses! As always, you can also replace seats (except for the pilot seat) with containers of your choosing. You can also place two additional containers at the stern.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Rowboats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat.json",
          "text": "In the rowboat, unlike other boats, you'll be sitting backwards when you're piloting the boat. It gives you a mechanical advantage so you can really put your back into those oars! You can, of course, pilot it in first-person, but if you may find it more intuitive in third-person facing yourself.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Rowboats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat.json",
          "text": "To build a rowboat, you'll need to start with Shipwright's Scaffolding. These allow you to keep the boat materials in place while you build. Then, you'll place them facing inward in a 2 by 3 area.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Rowboats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat.json",
          "text": "Rowboat Scaffolding",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.name",
          "label": "Rowboats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat.json",
          "text": "Shipwright's Scaffolding arranged in order to construct a rowboat.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Rowboats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat.json",
          "text": "Next, you'll need to add planks of suitably a strong hardwood: Acacia Ash Aspen Birch Blackwood Chestnut Hickory Maple Oak Rosewood Sycamore",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Rowboats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat.json",
          "text": "Rowboat Scaffolding",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.name",
          "label": "Rowboats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat.json",
          "text": "Four planks have been added to each scaffold.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Rowboats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat.json",
          "text": "Rowboat Scaffolding",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.name",
          "label": "Rowboats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat.json",
          "text": "Using a hammer in your offhand, add four copper bolts to each scaffold. Copper bolts are smithed from copper ingots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Rowboats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat.json",
          "text": "Rowboat Scaffolding",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.name",
          "label": "Rowboats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10
        }
      ],
